      <div class="sr_by_subd" id="stat.126C.16">
        <h1>126C.16 </h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.126C.16.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.</h2>
          <p>MS 2018 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/1/11/#laws.1.26.0">1Sp2019 c 11 art 1 s 26</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.126C.16.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/1/6/#laws.1.55.0">1Sp2001 c 6 art 1 s 55</a> subd 1]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.126C.16.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.</h2>
          <p>MS 2018 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/1/11/#laws.1.26.0">1Sp2019 c 11 art 1 s 26</a>]</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.126C.17">
        <h1 class="shn">126C.17 REFERENDUM REVENUE.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.126C.17.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Referendum allowance.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) A district's initial referendum allowance for fiscal year 2021 and later equals the result of the following calculations:</p>
          <p>(1) subtract $424 from the district's allowance under Minnesota Statutes 2018, section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/126C.17#stat.126C.17.1">126C.17, subdivision 1</a>, paragraph (a), clause (5);</p>
          <p>(2) if the result of clause (1) is less than zero, set the allowance to zero;</p>
          <p>(3) add to the result in clause (2) any new referendum allowance authorized between July 1, 2013, and December 31, 2013, under Minnesota Statutes 2013, section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/126C.17">126C.17</a>, subdivision 9a;</p>
          <p>(4) add to the result in clause (3) any additional referendum allowance per adjusted pupil unit authorized between January 1, 2014, and June 30, 2019;</p>
          <p>(5) subtract from the result in clause (4) any allowances expiring in fiscal year 2016, 2017, 2018, 2019, or 2020;</p>
          <p>(6) subtract $300 from the result in clause (5); and</p>
          <p>(7) if the result of clause (6) is less than zero, set the allowance to zero.</p>
          <p>(b) A district's referendum allowance equals the sum of the district's initial referendum allowance, plus any new referendum allowance authorized after July 1, 2019, minus any allowances expiring in fiscal year 2021 or later, plus any inflation adjustments for fiscal year 2021 and later approved by the voters prior to July 1, 2019, provided that the allowance may not be less than zero. For a district with more than one allowance for fiscal year 2015 that expires in the same year, the reduction under paragraph (a), clauses (1) and (6), shall be made first from any allowances that do not have an inflation adjustment approved by the voters.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.126C.17.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Referendum allowance limit.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Notwithstanding subdivision 1, for fiscal year 2021 and later, a district's referendum allowance must not exceed the greater of:</p>
          <p>(1) the product of the annual inflationary increase as calculated under paragraph (b), and $2,079.50, minus $300;</p>
          <p>(2) the product of the annual inflationary increase as calculated under paragraph (b), and the referendum allowance limit the district would have received for fiscal year 2021 under Minnesota Statutes 2018, section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/126C.17">126C.17, subdivision</a> 2, paragraph (a), clause (2), minus $300;</p>
          <p>(3) for a newly reorganized district created on July 1, 2020, the referendum revenue authority for each reorganizing district in the year preceding reorganization divided by its adjusted pupil units for the year preceding reorganization, minus $300; or</p>
          <p>(4) for a newly reorganized district created after July 1, 2020, the referendum revenue authority for each reorganizing district in the year preceding reorganization divided by its adjusted pupil units for the year preceding reorganization.</p>
          <p>(b) For purposes of this subdivision, for fiscal year 2022 and later, "inflationary increase" means one plus the percentage change in the Consumer Price Index for urban consumers, as prepared by the United States Bureau of Labor Statistics, for the current fiscal year to fiscal year 2021.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.126C.17.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Sparsity exception.</span></h2>
          <p>A district that qualifies for sparsity revenue under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/126C.10">126C.10</a> is not subject to a referendum allowance limit.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.126C.17.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Total referendum revenue.</span></h2>
          <p>The total referendum revenue for each district equals the district's referendum allowance times the adjusted pupil units for the school year.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.126C.17.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Referendum equalization revenue.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) A district's referendum equalization revenue equals the sum of the first tier referendum equalization revenue and the second tier referendum equalization revenue.</p>
          <p>(b) A district's first tier referendum equalization revenue equals the district's first tier referendum equalization allowance times the district's adjusted pupil units for that year.</p>
          <p>(c) A district's first tier referendum equalization allowance equals the lesser of the district's referendum allowance under subdivision 1 or $460.</p>
          <p>(d) A district's second tier referendum equalization revenue equals the district's second tier referendum equalization allowance times the district's adjusted pupil units for that year.</p>
          <p>(e) A district's second tier referendum equalization allowance equals the lesser of the district's referendum allowance under subdivision 1 or 25 percent of the formula allowance, minus the sum of $300 and the district's first tier referendum equalization allowance.</p>
          <p>(f) Notwithstanding paragraph (e), the second tier referendum equalization allowance for a district qualifying for secondary sparsity revenue under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/126C.10#stat.126C.10.7">126C.10, subdivision 7</a>, or elementary sparsity revenue under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/126C.10#stat.126C.10.8">126C.10, subdivision 8</a>, equals the district's referendum allowance under subdivision 1 minus the district's first tier referendum equalization allowance.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.126C.17.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Referendum equalization levy.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) A district's referendum equalization levy equals the sum of the first tier referendum equalization levy and the second tier referendum equalization levy.</p>
          <p>(b) A district's first tier referendum equalization levy equals the district's first tier referendum equalization revenue times the lesser of one or the ratio of the district's referendum market value per resident pupil unit to $567,000.</p>
          <p>(c) A district's second tier referendum equalization levy equals the district's second tier referendum equalization revenue times the lesser of one or the ratio of the district's referendum market value per resident pupil unit to $290,000.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.126C.17.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Referendum equalization aid.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) A district's referendum equalization aid equals the difference between its referendum equalization revenue and levy.</p>
          <p>(b) If a district's actual levy for first or second tier referendum equalization revenue is less than its maximum levy limit for that tier, aid shall be proportionately reduced.</p>
          <p>(c) Notwithstanding paragraph (a), the referendum equalization aid for a district must not exceed: (1) 25 percent of the formula allowance minus $300; times (2) the district's adjusted pupil units. A district's referendum levy is increased by the amount of any reduction in referendum aid under this paragraph.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.126C.17.7a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7a.<span class="headnote">Referendum tax base replacement aid.</span></h2>
          <p>For each school district that had a referendum allowance for fiscal year 2002 exceeding $415, for each separately authorized referendum levy, the commissioner of revenue, in consultation with the commissioner of education, shall certify the amount of the referendum levy in taxes payable year 2001 attributable to the portion of the referendum allowance exceeding $415 levied against property classified as class 2, noncommercial 4c(1), or 4c(4), under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.13">273.13</a>, excluding the portion of the tax paid by the portion of class 2a property consisting of the house, garage, and surrounding one acre of land. The resulting amount must be used to reduce the district's referendum levy or first tier local optional levy amount otherwise determined, and must be paid to the district each year that the referendum or first tier local optional authority remains in effect, is renewed, or new referendum authority is approved. The aid payable under this subdivision must be subtracted from the district's referendum equalization aid under subdivision 7. The referendum equalization aid and the first tier local optional aid after the subtraction must not be less than zero.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.126C.17.7b">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7b.<span class="headnote">Referendum aid guarantee.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Notwithstanding subdivision 7, the sum of a district's referendum equalization aid and local optional aid under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/126C.10">126C.10</a>, subdivision 2e, for fiscal year 2015 must not be less than the sum of the referendum equalization aid the district would have received for fiscal year 2015 under Minnesota Statutes 2012, section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/126C.17#stat.126C.17.7">126C.17, subdivision 7</a>, and the adjustment the district would have received under Minnesota Statutes 2012, section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/127A.47#stat.127A.47.7">127A.47, subdivision 7</a>, paragraphs (a), (b), and (c).</p>
          <p>(b) Notwithstanding subdivision 7, the sum of referendum equalization aid and local optional aid under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/126C.10#stat.126C.10.2e">126C.10, subdivision 2e</a>, for fiscal year 2016 and later, for a district qualifying for additional aid under paragraph (a) for fiscal year 2015, must not be less than the product of (1) the sum of the district's referendum equalization aid and local optional aid under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/126C.10#stat.126C.10.2e">126C.10, subdivision 2e</a>, for fiscal year 2015, times (2) the lesser of one or the ratio of the sum of the district's referendum revenue and local optional revenue for that school year to the sum of the district's referendum revenue and local optional revenue for fiscal year 2015, times (3) the lesser of one or the ratio of the district's referendum market value used for fiscal year 2015 referendum equalization calculations to the district's referendum market value used for that year's referendum equalization calculations.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.126C.17.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Unequalized referendum levy.</span></h2>
          <p>Each year, a district may levy an amount equal to the difference between its total referendum revenue according to subdivision 4 and its referendum equalization revenue according to subdivision 5.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.126C.17.9">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.<span class="headnote">Referendum revenue.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) The revenue authorized by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/126C.10#stat.126C.10.1">126C.10, subdivision 1</a>, may be increased in the amount approved by the voters of the district at a referendum called for the purpose. The referendum may be called by the board. The referendum must be conducted one or two calendar years before the increased levy authority, if approved, first becomes payable. Only one election to approve an increase may be held in a calendar year. Unless the referendum is conducted by mail under subdivision 11, paragraph (a), the referendum must be held on the first Tuesday after the first Monday in November. The ballot must state the maximum amount of the increased revenue per adjusted pupil unit. The ballot may state a schedule, determined by the board, of increased revenue per adjusted pupil unit that differs from year to year over the number of years for which the increased revenue is authorized or may state that the amount shall increase annually by the rate of inflation. For this purpose, the rate of inflation shall be the annual inflationary increase calculated under subdivision 2, paragraph (b). The ballot may state that existing referendum levy authority is expiring. In this case, the ballot may also compare the proposed levy authority to the existing expiring levy authority, and express the proposed increase as the amount, if any, over the expiring referendum levy authority. The ballot must designate the specific number of years, not to exceed ten, for which the referendum authorization applies. The ballot, including a ballot on the question to revoke or reduce the increased revenue amount under paragraph (c), must abbreviate the term "per adjusted pupil unit" as "per pupil." The notice required under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/275.60">275.60</a> may be modified to read, in cases of renewing existing levies at the same amount per pupil as in the previous year:</p>
          <p class="bi">"BY VOTING "YES" ON THIS BALLOT QUESTION, YOU ARE VOTING TO EXTEND AN EXISTING PROPERTY TAX REFERENDUM THAT IS SCHEDULED TO EXPIRE."</p>
          <p class="in">The ballot may contain a textual portion with the information required in this subdivision and a question stating substantially the following:</p>
          <p class="in">"Shall the increase in the revenue proposed by (petition to) the board of ......., School District No. .., be approved?"</p>
          <p class="in">If approved, an amount equal to the approved revenue per adjusted pupil unit times the adjusted pupil units for the school year beginning in the year after the levy is certified shall be authorized for certification for the number of years approved, if applicable, or until revoked or reduced by the voters of the district at a subsequent referendum.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) The board must deliver by mail at least 15 days but no more than 45 days before the day of the referendum to each taxpayer a notice of the referendum and the proposed revenue increase. The board need not mail more than one notice to any taxpayer. For the purpose of giving mailed notice under this subdivision, owners must be those shown to be owners on the records of the county auditor or, in any county where tax statements are mailed by the county treasurer, on the records of the county treasurer. Every property owner whose name does not appear on the records of the county auditor or the county treasurer is deemed to have waived this mailed notice unless the owner has requested in writing that the county auditor or county treasurer, as the case may be, include the name on the records for this purpose. The notice must project the anticipated amount of tax increase in annual dollars for typical residential homesteads, agricultural homesteads, apartments, and commercial-industrial property within the school district.</p>
          <p class="in">The notice for a referendum may state that an existing referendum levy is expiring and project the anticipated amount of increase over the existing referendum levy in the first year, if any, in annual dollars for typical residential homesteads, agricultural homesteads, apartments, and commercial-industrial property within the district.</p>
          <p class="in">The notice must include the following statement: "Passage of this referendum will result in an increase in your property taxes." However, in cases of renewing existing levies, the notice may include the following statement: "Passage of this referendum extends an existing operating referendum at the same amount per pupil as in the previous year."</p>
          <p class="in">(c) A referendum on the question of revoking or reducing the increased revenue amount authorized pursuant to paragraph (a) may be called by the board. A referendum to revoke or reduce the revenue amount must state the amount per adjusted pupil unit by which the authority is to be reduced. Revenue authority approved by the voters of the district pursuant to paragraph (a) must be available to the school district at least once before it is subject to a referendum on its revocation or reduction for subsequent years. Only one revocation or reduction referendum may be held to revoke or reduce referendum revenue for any specific year and for years thereafter.</p>
          <p class="in">(d) The approval of 50 percent plus one of those voting on the question is required to pass a referendum authorized by this subdivision.</p>
          <p class="in">(e) At least 15 days before the day of the referendum, the district must submit a copy of the notice required under paragraph (b) to the commissioner and to the county auditor of each county in which the district is located. Within 15 days after the results of the referendum have been certified by the board, or in the case of a recount, the certification of the results of the recount by the canvassing board, the district must notify the commissioner of the results of the referendum.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.126C.17.9a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9a.</h2>
          <p>MS 2018 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/1/11/#laws.1.26.0">1Sp2019 c 11 art 1 s 26</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.126C.17.9b">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9b.<span class="headnote">Renewal by school board.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Notwithstanding the election requirements of subdivision 9, a school board may renew an expiring referendum by board action if:</p>
          <p>(1) the per pupil amount of the referendum is the same as the amount expiring, or for an expiring referendum that was adjusted annually by the rate of inflation, the same as the per pupil amount of the expiring referendum, adjusted annually for inflation in the same manner as if the expiring referendum had continued;</p>
          <p>(2) the term of the renewed referendum is no longer than the initial term approved by the voters;</p>
          <p>(3) the school board, having taken a recorded vote, has adopted a written resolution authorizing the renewal after holding a meeting and allowing public testimony on the proposed renewal; and</p>
          <p>(4) the expiring referendum has not been previously renewed under this subdivision.</p>
          <p>(b) The resolution must be adopted by the school board by June 15 of any calendar year and becomes effective 60 days after its adoption.</p>
          <p>(c) A referendum expires in the last fiscal year in which the referendum generates revenue for the school district. A school board may renew an expiring referendum under this subdivision not more than two fiscal years before the referendum expires.</p>
          <p>(d) A district renewing an expiring referendum under this subdivision must submit a copy of the adopted resolution to the commissioner and to the county auditor no later than September 1 of the calendar year in which the written resolution is adopted.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.126C.17.10">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 10.<span class="headnote">School referendum levy; market value.</span></h2>
          <p>A school referendum levy must be levied against the referendum market value of all taxable property as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/126C.01#stat.126C.01.3">126C.01, subdivision 3</a>. Any referendum levy amount subject to the requirements of this subdivision must be certified separately to the county auditor under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/275.07">275.07</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.126C.17.11">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 11.<span class="headnote">Referendum date.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Except for a referendum held under paragraph (b), any referendum under this section held on a day other than the first Tuesday after the first Monday in November must be conducted by mail in accordance with section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/204B.46">204B.46</a>. Notwithstanding subdivision 9, paragraph (b), to the contrary, in the case of a referendum conducted by mail under this paragraph, the notice required by subdivision 9, paragraph (b), must be prepared and delivered by first-class mail at least 20 days before the referendum.</p>
          <p>(b) In addition to the referenda allowed in subdivision 9, paragraph (a), the commissioner may grant authority to a district to hold a referendum on a different day if the district is in statutory operating debt and has an approved plan or has received an extension from the department to file a plan to eliminate the statutory operating debt.</p>
          <p>(c) The commissioner must approve, deny, or modify each district's request for a referendum levy on a different day within 60 days of receiving the request from a district.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.126C.17.12">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 12.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/1/6/#laws.1.55.0">1Sp2001 c 6 art 1 s 55</a> subd 1]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.126C.17.13">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 13.</h2>
          <p>MS 2012 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/116/#laws.1.59.0">2013 c 116 art 1 s 59</a>]</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.126C.17.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>

      <div class="sr" id="stat.126C.18"><b>126C.18</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/1/6/#laws.1.55.0">1Sp2001 c 6 art 1 s 55</a> subd 1]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.126C.19">
        <h1 class="shn">126C.19 SHARED TIME AID.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.126C.19.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">To resident district.</span></h2>
          <p>Aid for shared time pupils must be paid to the district of the pupil's residence. If a pupil attends shared time classes in another district, the resident district must pay to the district of attendance an amount of tuition equal to the ratio in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/126C.01#stat.126C.01.6">126C.01, subdivision 6</a>, times the amount of tuition that would be charged and paid for a nonresident public school pupil in a similar circumstance. The district of residence is not obligated for tuition except by previous agreement.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.126C.19.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Exception.</span></h2>
          <p>Notwithstanding subdivision 1, the resident district of a shared time pupil attending shared time classes in another district or a charter school must grant the district or charter school of attendance, upon its request, permission to claim the pupil as a resident for state aid purposes. In this case, state aid must be paid to the district or charter school of attendance. If the resident district agrees, the district of attendance may bill the resident district for any unreimbursed education costs, but not for unreimbursed transportation costs. The resident district and the district or charter school of attendance may negotiate an agreement for the resident district to pay the cost of any of the particular transportation categories specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123B.92#stat.123B.92.1">123B.92, subdivision 1</a>, and in this case, aid for those categories must be paid to the district of residence rather than to the district of attendance.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.126C.19.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123B.44">123B.44</a> services. </span></h2>
          <p>Minutes of enrollment in a public school during which a nonpublic school pupil receives services pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123B.44">123B.44</a> must not be used in the computation of shared time aid.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.126C.19.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Location of services.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Public school programs that provide instruction in core curriculum may be provided to shared time pupils only at a public school building. Public school programs, excluding programs that provide instruction in core curriculum, may be provided to shared time pupils at a public school building, a neutral site, the nonpublic school, or any other suitable location. Guidance and counseling and diagnostic and health services required under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/125A.03">125A.03</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/125A.24">125A.24</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/125A.65">125A.65</a> may be provided at a nonpublic school building. As used in this subdivision, "diagnostic services" means speech, hearing, vision, psychological, medical and dental diagnostic services and "health services" means physician, nursing or optometric services provided to pupils in the field of physical and mental health.</p>
          <p>(b) For those children with a disability under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/125A.03">125A.03</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/125A.24">125A.24</a> who attend nonpublic school at their parent's choice, a school district may provide special instruction and services at the nonpublic school building, a public school, or at a neutral site other than a nonpublic school as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123B.41#stat.123B.41.13">123B.41, subdivision 13</a>. The school district shall determine the location at which to provide services on a student-by-student basis, consistent with federal law.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.126C.19.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/358/#laws.1.26.0">1981 c 358 art 1 s 26</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1983/0/314/#laws.1.5.0">1983 c 314 art 1 s 5</a>,22; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1988/0/486/#laws.0.51.0">1988 c 486 s 51</a>,52; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/265/#laws.3.38.0">1991 c 265 art 3 s 38</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/397/#laws.7.142.0">1998 c 397 art 7 s 142</a>,164; art 11 s 3; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/398/#laws.2.29.0">1998 c 398 art 2 s 29</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2012/0/239/#laws.1.26.0">2012 c 239 art 1 s 26</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.126C.20">
        <h1 class="shn">126C.20 ANNUAL GENERAL EDUCATION AID APPROPRIATION.</h1>
        <p>There is annually appropriated from the general fund to the department the amount necessary for general education aid under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/126C.13">126C.13</a>. This amount must be reduced by the amount of any money specifically appropriated for the same purpose in any year from any state fund.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.126C.20.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1961/0/562/#laws.0.14.0">1961 c 562 s 14</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1969/0/399/#laws.0.15.0">1969 c 399 s 15</a>,16; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/492/#laws.0.7.0">1973 c 492 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1975/0/432/#laws.0.20.0">1975 c 432 s 20</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1977/0/447/#laws.1.3.0">1977 c 447 art 1 s 3</a>; art 2 s 1; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1979/0/334/#laws.6.19.0">1979 c 334 art 6 s 19</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/358/#laws.7.23.0">1981 c 358 art 7 s 23</a>-26; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1982/0/548/#laws.7.4.0">1982 c 548 art 7 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1983/0/314/#laws.1.22.0">1983 c 314 art 1 s 22</a>; art 7 s 22; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/398/#laws.1.9.0">1987 c 398 art 1 s 9</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1988/0/486/#laws.0.50.0">1988 c 486 s 50</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/1/3/#laws.16.13.0">1Sp1995 c 3 art 16 s 13</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/397/#laws.7.141.0">1998 c 397 art 7 s 141</a>,164; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2011/1/11/#laws.1.22.0">1Sp2011 c 11 art 1 s 22</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/116/#laws.1.50.0">2013 c 116 art 1 s 50</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.126C.21">
        <h1 class="shn">126C.21 DEDUCTIONS FROM GENERAL EDUCATION AID.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.126C.21.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.</h2>
          <p>MS 2008 [Repealed, 2008 c 363 art 2 s 52]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.126C.21.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Minimum.</span></h2>
          <p>The amount payable to any district from state sources for any one year may not be reduced below the amount payable as apportionment of the school endowment fund pursuant to sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/127A.32">127A.32</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/127A.34">127A.34</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.126C.21.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">County apportionment deduction.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">Each year the amount of money apportioned to a district for that year pursuant to sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/127A.34#stat.127A.34.2">127A.34, subdivision 2</a>, and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/272.029#stat.272.029.6">272.029, subdivision 6</a>, must be deducted from the general education aid earned by that district for the same year or from aid earned from other state sources.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.126C.21.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/88/#laws.12.23.0">2009 c 88 art 12 s 23</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.126C.21.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Adjustment for failure to meet federal maintenance of effort.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) The general education aid paid to a school district or charter school that failed to meet federal special education maintenance of effort for the previous fiscal year must be reduced by the amount that must be paid to the federal government due to the shortfall.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) The general education aid paid to school districts that were members of a cooperative that failed to meet federal special education maintenance of effort must be reduced by the amount that must be paid to the federal government due to the shortfall. The commissioner must apportion the aid reduction amount to the member school districts based on each district's individual shortfall in maintaining effort, and on each member district's proportionate share of any shortfall in expenditures made by the cooperative. Each district's proportionate share of shortfall in expenditures made by the cooperative must be calculated using the adjusted marginal pupil units of each member school district.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) The amounts recovered under this subdivision shall be paid to the federal government to meet the state's obligations resulting from the district's, charter school's, or cooperative's failure to meet federal special education maintenance of effort.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.126C.21.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/358/#laws.1.27.0">1981 c 358 art 1 s 27</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1982/0/548/#laws.1.10.0">1982 c 548 art 1 s 10</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1983/0/314/#laws.1.6.0">1983 c 314 art 1 s 6</a>,17,22; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/268/#laws.9.5.0">1987 c 268 art 9 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1988/0/486/#laws.0.53.0">1988 c 486 s 53</a>-55; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1988/0/719/#laws.5.84.0">1988 c 719 art 5 s 84</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/329/#laws.13.20.0">1989 c 329 art 13 s 20</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/130/#laws.0.37.0">1991 c 130 s 37</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/499/#laws.12.29.0">1992 c 499 art 12 s 29</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1996/0/412/#laws.1.21.0">1996 c 412 art 1 s 21</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/397/#laws.7.143.0">1998 c 397 art 7 s 143</a>,164; art 11 s 3; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1999/0/86/#laws.1.34.0">1999 c 86 art 1 s 34</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/1/5/#laws.6.2.0">1Sp2001 c 5 art 6 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/377/#laws.8.1.0">2002 c 377 art 8 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/1/9/#laws.1.42.0">1Sp2003 c 9 art 1 s 42</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/5/#laws.1.34.0">1Sp2005 c 5 art 1 s 34</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2006/0/263/#laws.3.12.0">2006 c 263 art 3 s 12</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/146/#laws.1.12.0">2007 c 146 art 1 s 12</a>,13</p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.126C.22"><b>126C.22</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/1/6/#laws.1.55.0">1Sp2001 c 6 art 1 s 55</a> subd 1]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.126C.23"><b>126C.23</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/294/#laws.1.10.0">2004 c 294 art 1 s 10</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.126C.30"><b>126C.30</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/1/5/#laws.3.96.0">1Sp2001 c 5 art 3 s 96</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/1/6/#laws.1.55.0">1Sp2001 c 6 art 1 s 55</a> subd 1]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.126C.31"><b>126C.31</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/1/5/#laws.3.96.0">1Sp2001 c 5 art 3 s 96</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/1/6/#laws.1.55.0">1Sp2001 c 6 art 1 s 55</a> subd 1]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.126C.32"><b>126C.32</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/1/5/#laws.3.96.0">1Sp2001 c 5 art 3 s 96</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/1/6/#laws.1.55.0">1Sp2001 c 6 art 1 s 55</a> subd 1]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.126C.33"><b>126C.33</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/1/5/#laws.3.96.0">1Sp2001 c 5 art 3 s 96</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/1/6/#laws.1.55.0">1Sp2001 c 6 art 1 s 55</a> subd 1]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.126C.34"><b>126C.34</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/1/5/#laws.3.96.0">1Sp2001 c 5 art 3 s 96</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/1/6/#laws.1.55.0">1Sp2001 c 6 art 1 s 55</a> subd 1]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.126C.35"><b>126C.35</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/1/5/#laws.3.96.0">1Sp2001 c 5 art 3 s 96</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/1/6/#laws.1.55.0">1Sp2001 c 6 art 1 s 55</a> subd 1]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.126C.36"><b>126C.36</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/1/5/#laws.3.96.0">1Sp2001 c 5 art 3 s 96</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/1/6/#laws.1.55.0">1Sp2001 c 6 art 1 s 55</a> subd 1]</div>
      <h1 class="fgh">LEVIES; NONPROGRAM SPECIFIC</h1>
      <div class="section" id="stat.126C.40">
        <h1 class="shn">126C.40 CAPITAL LEVIES.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.126C.40.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">To lease building or land.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) When an independent or a special school district or a group of independent or special school districts finds it economically advantageous to rent or lease a building or land for any instructional purposes or for school storage or furniture repair, and it determines that the operating capital revenue authorized under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/126C.10#stat.126C.10.13">126C.10, subdivision 13</a>, is insufficient for this purpose, it may apply to the commissioner for permission to make an additional capital expenditure levy for this purpose. An application for permission to levy under this subdivision must contain financial justification for the proposed levy, the terms and conditions of the proposed lease, and a description of the space to be leased and its proposed use.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) The criteria for approval of applications to levy under this subdivision must include: the reasonableness of the price, the appropriateness of the space to the proposed activity, the feasibility of transporting pupils to the leased building or land, conformity of the lease to the laws and rules of the state of Minnesota, and the appropriateness of the proposed lease to the space needs and the financial condition of the district. The commissioner must not authorize a levy under this subdivision in an amount greater than the cost to the district of renting or leasing a building or land for approved purposes. The proceeds of this levy must not be used for custodial or other maintenance services. A district may not levy under this subdivision for the purpose of leasing or renting a district-owned building or site to itself.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) For agreements finalized after July 1, 1997, a district may not levy under this subdivision for the purpose of leasing: (1) a newly constructed building used primarily for regular kindergarten, elementary, or secondary instruction; or (2) a newly constructed building addition or additions used primarily for regular kindergarten, elementary, or secondary instruction that contains more than 20 percent of the square footage of the previously existing building.</p>
          <p class="in">(d) Notwithstanding paragraph (b), a district may levy under this subdivision for the purpose of leasing or renting a district-owned building or site to itself only if the amount is needed by the district to make payments required by a lease purchase agreement, installment purchase agreement, or other deferred payments agreement authorized by law, and the levy meets the requirements of paragraph (c). A levy authorized for a district by the commissioner under this paragraph may be in the amount needed by the district to make payments required by a lease purchase agreement, installment purchase agreement, or other deferred payments agreement authorized by law, provided that any agreement include a provision giving the school districts the right to terminate the agreement annually without penalty.</p>
          <p class="in">(e) The total levy under this subdivision for a district for any year must not exceed $212 times the adjusted pupil units for the fiscal year to which the levy is attributable.</p>
          <p class="in">(f) For agreements for which a review and comment have been submitted to the Department of Education after April 1, 1998, the term "instructional purpose" as used in this subdivision excludes expenditures on stadiums.</p>
          <p class="in">(g) The commissioner of education may authorize a school district to exceed the limit in paragraph (e) if the school district petitions the commissioner for approval. The commissioner shall grant approval to a school district to exceed the limit in paragraph (e) for not more than five years if the district meets the following criteria:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) the school district has been experiencing pupil enrollment growth in the preceding five years;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) the purpose of the increased levy is in the long-term public interest;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) the purpose of the increased levy promotes colocation of government services; and</p>
          <p class="in">(4) the purpose of the increased levy is in the long-term interest of the district by avoiding over construction of school facilities.</p>
          <p class="in">(h) A school district that is a member of an intermediate school district or other cooperative unit under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123A.24#stat.123A.24.2">123A.24, subdivision 2</a>, or a joint powers district under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/471.59">471.59</a> may include in its authority under this section the costs associated with leases of administrative and classroom space for programs of the intermediate school district or other cooperative unit under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123A.24">123A.24, subdivision </a>2, or joint powers district under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/471.59">471.59</a>. This authority must not exceed $65 times the adjusted pupil units of the member districts. This authority is in addition to any other authority authorized under this section. The intermediate school district, other cooperative unit, or joint powers district may specify which member districts will levy for lease costs under this paragraph.</p>
          <p>(i) Notwithstanding paragraph (a), a district may levy under this subdivision for the purpose of leasing administrative space if the district can demonstrate to the satisfaction of the commissioner that the lease cost for the administrative space is no greater than the lease cost for instructional space that the district would otherwise lease. The commissioner must deny this levy authority unless the district passes a resolution stating its intent to lease instructional space under this section if the commissioner does not grant authority under this paragraph. The resolution must also certify that the lease cost for administrative space under this paragraph is no greater than the lease cost for the district's proposed instructional lease.</p>
          <p>(j) Notwithstanding paragraph (a), a district may levy under this subdivision for the district's proportionate share of deferred maintenance expenditures for a district-owned building or site leased to a cooperative unit under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123A.24#stat.123A.24.2">123A.24, subdivision 2</a>, or a joint powers district under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/471.59">471.59</a> for any instructional purposes or for school storage.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.126C.40.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Pre-July 1990 lease purchase, installment buys.</span></h2>
          <p>A district may annually levy the amount needed to make payments required by a lease purchase agreement, installment purchase agreement, or other deferred payment agreement authorized by Minnesota Statutes 1989 Supplement, section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/465.71">465.71</a>, if:</p>
          <p>(1) the agreement was approved by the commissioner before July 1, 1990, according to Minnesota Statutes 1989 Supplement, section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/275.125#stat.275.125.11d">275.125, subdivision 11d</a>; or</p>
          <p>(2) the district levied in 1989 for the payments.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.126C.40.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Cooperating districts.</span></h2>
          <p>A district that has an agreement according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123A.30">123A.30</a> or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123A.32">123A.32</a> may levy for the repair costs, as approved by the department of a building located in another district that is a party to the agreement.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.126C.40.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.</h2>
          <p>[Expired]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.126C.40.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Energy conservation.</span></h2>
          <p> For energy loans approved after March 1, 1998, under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/216C.37">216C.37</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/298.292">298.292</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/298.298">298.298</a>, school districts must annually transfer from the general fund to the debt redemption fund the amount sufficient to pay interest and principal on the loans.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.126C.40.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Lease purchase; installment buys.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Upon application to, and approval by, the commissioner in accordance with the procedures and limits in subdivision 1, paragraphs (a) and (b), a district, as defined in this subdivision, may:</p>
          <p>(1) purchase real or personal property under an installment contract or may lease real or personal property with an option to purchase under a lease purchase agreement, by which installment contract or lease purchase agreement title is kept by the seller or vendor or assigned to a third party as security for the purchase price, including interest, if any; and</p>
          <p>(2) annually levy the amounts necessary to pay the district's obligations under the installment contract or lease purchase agreement.</p>
          <p>(b) The obligation created by the installment contract or the lease purchase agreement must not be included in the calculation of net debt for purposes of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/475.53">475.53</a>, and does not constitute debt under other law. An election is not required in connection with the execution of the installment contract or the lease purchase agreement.</p>
          <p>(c) The proceeds of the levy authorized by this subdivision must not be used to acquire a facility to be primarily used for athletic or school administration purposes.</p>
          <p>(d) For the purposes of this subdivision, "district" means:</p>
          <p>(1) Special School District No. 1, Minneapolis, Independent School District No. 625, St. Paul, Independent School District No. 709, Duluth, or Independent School District No. 535, Rochester, if the district's achievement and integration plan has been determined by the commissioner to be in compliance with Department of Education rules relating to equality of educational opportunity and where the acquisition, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/475.51#stat.475.51.7">475.51, subdivision 7</a>, of property under this subdivision is approved in the form and manner prescribed by the commissioner to contribute to the implementation of the approved achievement and integration plan; or</p>
          <p>(2) other districts eligible for revenue under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/124D.862">124D.862</a> if the facility acquired under this subdivision is to be primarily used for a joint program the commissioner determines is being undertaken to implement the districts' approved achievement and integration plan.</p>
          <p>(e) Notwithstanding subdivision 1, the prohibition against a levy by a district to lease or rent a district-owned building to itself does not apply to levies otherwise authorized by this subdivision.</p>
          <p>(f) For the purposes of this subdivision, any references in subdivision 1 to building or land shall include personal property.</p>
          <p>(g) Projects funded under this subdivision that require an expenditure in excess of $500,000 per school site if the school district has a capital loan outstanding, or $2,000,000 per school site if the school district does not have a capital loan outstanding, are subject to review and comment under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123B.71#stat.123B.71.8">123B.71, subdivision 8</a>, in the form and manner prescribed by the commissioner. Provided no referendum for bonds is required, the school board must discuss the commissioner's determination of a review and comment and the district's approved achievement and integration plan findings at a regular or special school board meeting within 45 days of the commissioner's determination. A school board's failure to comply with the discussion requirement in this paragraph shall not otherwise affect the legality, validity, or binding nature of any school district action or obligation not subject to referendum.</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="section" id="stat.126C.41">
        <h1 class="shn">126C.41 BENEFITS LEVIES.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.126C.41.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2015/1/3/#laws.1.28.0">1Sp2015 c 3 art 1 s 28</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.126C.41.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Retired employee health benefits.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) A district may levy an amount up to the amount the district is required by the collective bargaining agreement in effect on March 30, 1992, to pay for health insurance or unreimbursed medical expenses for licensed and nonlicensed employees who have terminated services in the employing district and withdrawn from active teaching service or other active service, as applicable, before July 1, 1992, and to pay for health insurance or unreimbursed medical expenses for licensed and nonlicensed employees who have terminated services in the employing district and withdrawn from active teaching service or other active service, as applicable before July 1, 1998, only if a sunset clause is in effect for the current collective bargaining agreement. The total amount of the levy each year may not exceed $600,000.</p>
          <p>(b) In addition to the levy authority granted under paragraph (a), a school district may levy for other postemployment benefits expenses actually paid during the previous fiscal year. For purposes of this subdivision, "postemployment benefits" means benefits giving rise to a liability under Statement No. 45 of the Government Accounting Standards Board. A district seeking levy authority under this subdivision must:</p>
          <p>(1) create or have created an actuarial liability to pay postemployment benefits to employees or officers after their termination of service;</p>
          <p>(2) have a sunset clause in effect for the current collective bargaining agreement as required by paragraph (a); and</p>
          <p>(3) apply for the authority in the form and manner required by the commissioner of education.</p>
          <p class="bl">If the total levy authority requested under this paragraph exceeds the amount established in paragraph (c), the commissioner must proportionately reduce each district's maximum levy authority under this subdivision. The commissioner may subsequently adjust each district's levy authority under this subdivision so long as the total levy authority does not exceed the maximum levy authority for that year.</p>
          <p>(c) The maximum levy authority under paragraph (b) must not exceed the following amounts:</p>
          <p>(1) $9,242,000 for taxes payable in 2010;</p>
          <p>(2) $29,863,000 for taxes payable in 2011; and</p>
          <p>(3) for taxes payable in 2012 and later, the maximum levy authority must not exceed the sum of the previous year's authority and $14,000,000.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.126C.41.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Retirement levies.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) In 1991 and each year thereafter, a district to which this subdivision applies may levy an additional amount required for contributions to the general employees retirement plan of the Public Employees Retirement Association as the successor of the Minneapolis Employees Retirement Fund as a result of the maximum dollar amount limitation on state contributions to that plan imposed under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/353.505">353.505</a>. The additional levy must not exceed the most recent amount certified by the executive director of the Public Employees Retirement Association as the district's share of the contribution requirement in excess of the maximum state contribution under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/353.505">353.505</a>.</p>
          <p>(b) For taxes payable in 1994 and thereafter, Special School District No. 1, Minneapolis, and Independent School District No. 625, St. Paul, may levy for the increase in the employer retirement fund contributions, under Laws 1992, chapter 598, article 5, section 1.</p>
          <p>(c) If the employer retirement fund contributions under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354A.12#stat.354A.12.2a">354A.12, subdivision 2a</a>, are increased for fiscal year 1994 or later fiscal years, Special School District No. 1, Minneapolis, and Independent School District No. 625, St. Paul, may levy in payable 1994 or later an amount equal to the amount derived by applying the net increase in the employer retirement fund contribution rate of the respective teacher retirement fund association between fiscal year 1993 and the fiscal year beginning in the year after the levy is certified to the total covered payroll of the applicable teacher retirement fund association. If an applicable school district levies under this paragraph, they may not levy under paragraph (b).</p>
          <p>(d) In addition to the levy authorized under paragraph (c), Special School District No. 1, Minneapolis, may also levy payable in 1997 or later an amount equal to the contributions under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.435#stat.354.435.2">354.435, subdivision 2</a>, and may also levy in payable 1994 or later an amount equal to the state aid contribution under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.435#stat.354.435.1">354.435, subdivision 1</a>. Independent School District No. 625, St. Paul, may levy payable in 1997 or later an amount equal to the supplemental contributions under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/423A.02#stat.423A.02.3">423A.02, subdivision 3</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.126C.41.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Minneapolis health insurance subsidy.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">Each year Special School District No. 1, Minneapolis, may make an additional levy not to exceed the amount raised by a net tax rate of .10 percent times the adjusted net tax capacity for taxes payable in 1991 and thereafter of the property in the district for the preceding year. The proceeds may be used only to subsidize health insurance costs for eligible teachers as provided in this section.</p>
          <p class="in">"Eligible teacher" means a retired teacher who is a retired member of the Teachers Retirement Association, who was a basic member of the former Minneapolis Teachers Retirement Fund Association, who retired before May 1, 1974, or who had 20 or more years of basic member service in the former Minneapolis Teachers Retirement Fund Association and retired before June 30, 1983, and who is not eligible to receive the hospital insurance benefits of the federal Medicare program of the Social Security Act without payment of a monthly premium. The district must notify eligible teachers that a subsidy is available. To obtain a subsidy, an eligible teacher must submit to the school district a copy of receipts for health insurance premiums paid. The district must disburse the health insurance premium subsidy to each eligible teacher according to a schedule determined by the district, but at least annually. An eligible teacher may receive a subsidy up to an amount equal to the lesser of 90 percent of the cost of the eligible teacher's health insurance or up to 90 percent of the cost of the number two qualified plan of health coverage for individual policies made available by the Minnesota comprehensive health association under chapter 62E.</p>
          <p class="in">If funds remaining from the previous year's health insurance subsidy levy, minus the previous year's required subsidy amount, are sufficient to pay the estimated current year subsidy, the levy must be discontinued until the remaining funds are estimated by the school board to be insufficient to pay the subsidy.</p>
          <p class="in">This subdivision does not extend benefits to teachers who retire after June 30, 1983, and does not create a contractual right or claim for altering the benefits in this subdivision. This subdivision does not restrict the district's right to modify or terminate coverage under this subdivision.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.126C.41.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">St. Paul severance levy.</span></h2>
          <p>The school board of Independent School District No. 625, St. Paul, for the purpose of providing moneys for the payment of its severance pay obligations under a plan approved by resolution of the district, in addition to all other powers possessed by the school district and in addition to and in excess of any existing limitation upon the amount it is otherwise authorized by law to levy as taxes, is authorized to levy taxes annually not exceeding in any one year an amount equal to a net tax capacity rate of 0.36 percent for taxes payable in 2002 and thereafter upon all taxable property within the school district which taxes as levied shall be spread upon the tax rolls, and all corrections thereof shall be held by the school district, and allocated therefor to be disbursed and expended by the school district in payment of any public school severance pay obligations and for no other purpose. Disbursements and expenditures previously authorized on behalf of the school district for payment of severance pay obligations shall not be deemed to constitute any part of the cost of the operation and maintenance of the school district within the meaning of any statutory limitation of any school district expenditures.</p>
          <p>The amount of such severance pay allowable or to become payable in respect of any such employment or to any such employee shall not exceed the amount permitted by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/465.72">465.72</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.126C.41.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Levy authority for unfunded severance and retirement costs.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) A school district qualifies for eligibility under this section if the district:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) participated in the cooperative secondary facilities program;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) consolidated with at least two other school districts; and</p>
          <p class="in">(3) has unfunded severance or retirement costs.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) An eligible school district may annually levy up to $150,000 for unfunded severance or retirement costs. This levy authority expires after taxes payable in 2017.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) A school district that levies under this section must reserve the proceeds of the levy and spend those amounts only for unfunded severance or retirement costs.</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="sr_by_subd" id="stat.126C.42">
        <h1>126C.42 </h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.126C.42.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/5/#laws.1.55.0">1Sp2005 c 5 art 1 s 55</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.126C.42.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/1/6/#laws.1.55.0">1Sp2001 c 6 art 1 s 55</a> subd 1]</p>
        </div>

      <div class="section" id="stat.126C.43">
        <h1 class="shn">126C.43 LEVIES; STATUTORY OBLIGATIONS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.126C.43.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Allocation of assets and liabilities.</span></h2>
          <p>A district may levy the amount authorized for liabilities of dissolved districts pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123A.67">123A.67</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.126C.43.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Payment to unemployment insurance program trust fund by state and political subdivisions.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) A district may levy the amount necessary (1) to pay the district's obligations under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/268.052#stat.268.052.1">268.052, subdivision 1</a>, and (2) to pay for job placement services offered to employees who may become eligible for benefits pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/268.085">268.085</a> for the fiscal year the levy is certified.</p>
          <p>(b) Districts with a balance remaining in their reserve for reemployment as of June 30, 2003, may not expend the reserved funds for future reemployment expenditures. Each year a levy reduction must be made to return these funds to taxpayers. The amount of the levy reduction must be equal to the lesser of: (1) the remaining reserved balance for reemployment; or (2) the amount of the district's current levy under paragraph (a).</p>
          <p>(c) The amount in paragraph (a) must not include the amounts for hourly school employees during the period of the summer term.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.126C.43.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Tax levy for judgment.</span></h2>
          <p>A district may levy the amount necessary to pay judgments against the district under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123B.25">123B.25</a> that became final after the date the district certified its proposed levy in the previous year. With the approval of the commissioner, a district may spread this levy over a period not to exceed three years. Upon approval through the adoption of a resolution by each of an intermediate district's member school district boards, a member school district may include its proportionate share of the costs of a judgment against an intermediate school district that became final under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123B.25">123B.25</a> after the date that the earliest member school district certified its proposed levy in the previous year. With the approval of the commissioner, an intermediate school district member school district may spread this levy over a period not to exceed three years.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.126C.43.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Levy limitations of reorganized districts.</span></h2>
          <p>A district may levy the amounts authorized by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123A.73">123A.73</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.126C.43.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Expenses of transition; dissolved district.</span></h2>
          <p>A district may levy the amounts necessary to pay the district's obligations under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123A.76">123A.76</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.126C.43.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Teacher severance pay.</span></h2>
          <p>A district may levy for severance pay required by sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/124D.05#stat.124D.05.3">124D.05, subdivision 3</a>, and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123A.30#stat.123A.30.6">123A.30, subdivision 6</a>.</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="section" id="stat.126C.44">
        <h1 class="shn">126C.44 SAFE SCHOOLS REVENUE.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.126C.44.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Safe schools revenue for school districts.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">A school district's safe schools revenue equals its safe schools levy.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.126C.44.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">School district safe schools levy.</span></h2>
          <p>A school district's safe schools levy equals $36 times the district's adjusted pupil units for the school year.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.126C.44.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Safe schools revenue for intermediate school districts.</span></h2>
          <p>A school district that is a member of an intermediate school district may include in its levy authority under this section the costs associated with safe schools activities authorized under this section for intermediate school district programs. This authority must not exceed the product of $15 and the adjusted pupil units of the member districts. This authority is in addition to any other authority authorized under this section. Revenue raised under this subdivision must be transferred to the intermediate school district.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.126C.44.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Use of safe schools revenue.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) Safe schools revenue must be reserved and used for directly funding the following purposes or for reimbursing the cities and counties who contract with the district for the following purposes:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) to pay the costs incurred for the salaries, benefits, and transportation costs of peace officers and sheriffs for liaison in services in the district's schools;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) to pay the costs for a drug abuse prevention program as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.101#stat.609.101.3">609.101, subdivision 3</a>, paragraph (e), in the elementary schools;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) to pay the costs for a gang resistance education training curriculum in the district's schools;</p>
          <p class="in">(4) to pay the costs for security in the district's schools and on school property;</p>
          <p class="in">(5) to pay the costs for other crime prevention, drug abuse, student and staff safety, voluntary opt-in suicide prevention tools, and violence prevention measures taken by the school district;</p>
          <p class="in">(6) to pay costs for licensed school counselors, licensed school nurses, licensed school social workers, licensed school psychologists, and licensed alcohol and substance use disorder counselors to help provide early responses to problems;</p>
          <p class="in">(7) to pay for facility security enhancements including laminated glass, public announcement systems, emergency communications devices, and equipment and facility modifications related to violence prevention and facility security;</p>
          <p class="in">(8) to pay for costs associated with improving the school climate;</p>
          <p class="in">(9) to pay costs for colocating and collaborating with mental health professionals who are not district employees or contractors; or</p>
          <p class="in">(10) to pay for the costs of cybersecurity measures, including updating computer hardware and software, other systems upgrades, and cybersecurity insurance costs.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) For expenditures under paragraph (a), clause (1), the district must initially attempt to contract for services to be provided by peace officers or sheriffs with the police department of each city or the sheriff's department of the county within the district containing the school receiving the services. If a local police department or a county sheriff's department does not wish to provide the necessary services, the district may contract for these services with any other police or sheriff's department located entirely or partially within the school district's boundaries.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.126C.44.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>

art 2 s 31; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/397/#laws.7.112.0">1998 c 397 art 7 s 112</a>,164; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1999/0/241/#laws.2.48.0">1999 c 241 art 2 s 48</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/254/#laws.0.44.0">2000 c 254 s 44</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/489/#laws.2.24.0">2000 c 489 art 2 s 24</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/377/#laws.5.4.0">2002 c 377 art 5 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/1/9/#laws.2.38.0">1Sp2003 c 9 art 2 s 38</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2006/0/263/#laws.1.15.0">2006 c 263 art 1 s 15</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/146/#laws.1.14.0">2007 c 146 art 1 s 14</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/363/#laws.2.24.0">2008 c 363 art 2 s 24</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/96/#laws.1.17.0">2009 c 96 art 1 s 17</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2011/1/11/#laws.1.24.0">1Sp2011 c 11 art 1 s 24</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/55/#laws.8.15.0">2023 c 55 art 8 s 15</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.126C.445">
        <h1 class="shn">126C.445 TREE GROWTH REPLACEMENT REVENUE.</h1>
        <p>For taxes payable in 2003 and later, a school district may levy an amount not to exceed its miscellaneous revenue for tree growth revenue for taxes payable in 2001.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.126C.445.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/377/#laws.6.2.0">2002 c 377 art 6 s 2</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.126C.45">
        <h1 class="shn">126C.45 ICE ARENA LEVY.</h1>
        <p class="in">(a) Each year, an independent school district operating and maintaining an ice arena, may levy for the net operational costs of the ice arena. The levy may not exceed the net actual costs of operation of the arena for the previous year. Net actual costs are defined as operating costs less any operating revenues.</p>
        <p class="in">(b) Any district operating and maintaining an ice arena must demonstrate to the satisfaction of the Office of Monitoring in the department that the district will offer equal sports opportunities for male and female students to use its ice arena, particularly in areas of access to prime practice time, team support, and providing junior varsity and younger level teams for girls' ice sports and ice sports offerings.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.126C.45.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1975/0/432/#laws.0.75.0">1975 c 432 s 75</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1976/0/271/#laws.0.81.0">1976 c 271 s 81</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1977/0/447/#laws.6.8.0">1977 c 447 art 6 s 8</a>;       </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.126C.455">
        <h1 class="shn">126C.455 SWIMMING POOL LEVY.</h1>
        <p>Each year, a school district with its home office located in a county that has (i) a population density of ten or fewer persons per square mile according to the 2000 census of population; (ii) an international border; and (iii) more than one school district within its boundaries, may levy for the net operational costs of a swimming pool. The levy may not exceed the net actual costs of operation of the swimming pool for the previous year. Net actual costs are defined as operating costs less any operating revenues and less any payments from other local governmental units.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.126C.455.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/1/5/#laws.3.7.0">1Sp2001 c 5 art 3 s 7</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.126C.457"><b>126C.457</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2011/1/11/#laws.1.37.0">1Sp2011 c 11 art 1 s 37</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.126C.46">
        <h1 class="shn">126C.46 ABATEMENT LEVY.</h1>
        <p>(a) Each year, a school district may levy an amount to replace the net revenue lost to abatements that have occurred under chapter 278, section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/270C.86">270C.86</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/375.192">375.192</a>, or otherwise. The maximum abatement levy is the sum of:</p>
        <p>(1) the amount of the net revenue loss determined under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/127A.49#stat.127A.49.2">127A.49, subdivision 2</a>, that is not paid in state aid including any aid amounts not paid due to proration;</p>
        <p>(2) the difference of (i) the amount of any abatements that have been reported by the county auditor for the first six months of the calendar year during which the abatement levy is certified that the district chooses to levy, (ii) less any amount actually levied under this clause that was certified in the previous calendar year for the first six months of the previous calendar year; and</p>
        <p>(3) an amount equal to any interest paid on abatement refunds.</p>
        <p>(b) A district may spread this levy over a period not to exceed two years. With the approval of the commissioner, a district may spread this levy over a period not to exceed three years.</p>
        <p>By July 15, the county auditor shall separately report the abatements that have occurred during the first six calendar months of that year to the commissioner and each district located within the county.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.126C.46.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1975/0/432/#laws.0.75.0">1975 c 432 s 75</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1976/0/271/#laws.0.81.0">1976 c 271 s 81</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1977/0/447/#laws.6.8.0">1977 c 447 art 6 s 8</a>;
